<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-47522709" data-id="47522709" data-tags="Privacy|Politics|Security" data-score="1429" data-comments="387" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47522709" data-time="1774470423">
<a class="thumb" href="https://fightchatcontrol.eu/?foo=bar" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=fightchatcontrol.eu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>fightchatcontrol.eu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fightchatcontrol.eu/?foo=bar" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The EU still wants to scan  your private messages and photos</span><span class='zh'>欧盟仍欲对私人信息与照片进行扫描</span></a><span class="dom">fightchatcontrol.eu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Despite pushback, the EU continues to pursue scanning private messages and photos as part of its safety agenda.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟尽管遭到反对，仍在推动对私人信息与照片进行扫描，作为安全与反恐的一部分。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Despite pushback, the EU continues to pursue scanning private messages and photos as part of its safety agenda. The plan raises concerns about encryption, privacy, and the trade-offs between surveillance and individual rights.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟尽管遭到反对，仍在推动对私人信息与照片进行扫描，作为安全与反恐的一部分。该举动引发关于端对端加密、隐私保护与政府监控边界的担忧与争论。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='Politics' style='--c:#f97316'>Politics</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>1429</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47522709' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>387</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47556729" data-id="47556729" data-tags="Health|Startups|Energy" data-score="1343" data-comments="248" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47556729" data-time="1774719589">
<a class="thumb" href="https://sytse.com/cancer/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://sytse.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/Screenshot-2026-01-15-at-12.59.23-PM-967x1024.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sytse.com' data-c='#4ade80' data-tag='Health'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sytse.com/cancer/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Founder of GitLab battles cancer by founding companies</span><span class='zh'>GitLab 创始人通过创办新公司对抗癌症</span></a><span class="dom">sytse.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitLab&#x27;s cofounder is battling cancer and continues pushing forward by launching new companies.</span><span class='zh'>GitLab 联合创始人正在与癌症作斗争，但仍通过创办新公司继续前进。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitLab&#x27;s cofounder is battling cancer and continues pushing forward by launching new companies. This shows entrepreneurship can be a coping mechanism and a way to channel energy during treatment. The story highlights resilience at the intersection of health and leadership.</span><span class='zh'>GitLab 联合创始人正在与癌症作斗争，但仍通过创办新公司继续前进。这一做法凸显创业在治疗期间用以应对压力的作用，以及在健康挑战中保持生产力的能力。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.xda-developers.com/wine-11-rewrites-linux-runs-windows-games-speed-gains/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://static0.xdaimages.com/wordpress/wp-content/uploads/wm/2025/09/windows-apps-through-wine.jpg?w=1600&amp;h=900&amp;fit=crop' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='xda-developers.com' data-c='#a855f7' data-tag='Gaming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">03</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.xda-developers.com/wine-11-rewrites-linux-runs-windows-games-speed-gains/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Wine 11 rewrites how Linux runs Windows games at kernel with massive speed gains</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11：在内核层重写以显著提升 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的速度</span></a><span class="dom">xda-developers.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wine 11 overhauls kernel interactions to accelerate Windows games on Linux, delivering substantial speedups; it improves compatibility and reduces overhead in system calls translation and graphics handling.</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11 对内核层进行重写，使 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的性能显著提升，并提高了兼容性与系统调用翻译、图形处理的效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wine 11 overhauls kernel interactions to accelerate Windows games on Linux, delivering substantial speedups; it improves compatibility and reduces overhead in system calls translation and graphics handling. This move tightens the gap between Linux gaming and Windows performance, potentially expanding Linux gaming adoption.</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11 对内核层进行重写，使 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的性能显著提升，并提高了兼容性与系统调用翻译、图形处理的效率。此举缩小了 Linux 与 Windows 在游戏性能上的差距，有望推动 Linux 游戏生态的扩展。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mariozechner.at/posts/2026-03-25-thoughts-on-slowing-the-fuck-down/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Thoughts on slowing the fuck down</span><span class='zh'>放慢脚步的一些思考</span></a><span class="dom">mariozechner.at</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Slowing down can protect long-term quality and prevent burnout in fast-moving tech work.</span><span class='zh'>放慢节奏或许比盲目追求速度更能保障长期产出质量，避免职业倦怠。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Slowing down can protect long-term quality and prevent burnout in fast-moving tech work. The piece argues for deliberate pacing, better boundaries, and smarter prioritization to sustain creativity and reliability over time.</span><span class='zh'>放慢节奏或许比盲目追求速度更能保障长期产出质量，避免职业倦怠。文中倡导有意识的节奏控制、设定边界以及更聪明的优先级排序，以维持创造力和可靠性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://www.sambent.com/microsofts-plan-to-fix-windows-11-is-gaslighting/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.sambent.com/content/images/size/w1200/2026/03/microsoft-plan-fix-windows-11-gaslighting-1.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='sambent.com' data-c='#3b82f6' data-tag='Programming'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sambent.com/microsofts-plan-to-fix-windows-11-is-gaslighting/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft&#x27;s &quot;fix&quot; for Windows 11</span><span class='zh'>微软对 Windows 11 的“修复”：挨打后的花束</span></a><span class="dom">sambent.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft continues to roll out patches for Windows 11, but critics argue the changes are cosmetic and don&#x27;t address core reliability or usability issues.</span><span class='zh'>微软继续推出 Windows 11 的补丁，但批评者认为这些改动多为表面修补，无法解决核心的可靠性或可用性问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft continues to roll out patches for Windows 11, but critics argue the changes are cosmetic and don&#x27;t address core reliability or usability issues. The messaging resembles a post-incident PR sprint rather than substantive engineering fixes. For tech readers, this highlights the gap between marketing narratives and real-world OS quality in major updates.</span><span class='zh'>微软继续推出 Windows 11 的补丁，但批评者认为这些改动多为表面修补，无法解决核心的可靠性或可用性问题。外部宣传更像是事后公关冲刺，而非实质工程改进。对技术读者而言，这凸显了大型操作系统更新中营销叙述与实际软件质量之间的差距。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>1023</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47500335' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>738</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story top" id="story-47518960" data-id="47518960" data-tags="Design" data-score="1006" data-comments="200" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47518960" data-time="1774453589">
<a class="thumb" href="https://bethmathews.substack.com/p/why-so-many-control-rooms-were-seafoam" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#f472b6' data-tag='Design'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=bethmathews.substack.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>bethmathews.substack.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bethmathews.substack.com/p/why-so-many-control-rooms-were-seafoam" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why so many control rooms were seafoam green (2025)</span><span class='zh'>为什么大量控制室偏好海泡绿（2025）</span></a><span class="dom">bethmathews.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The color choice in control rooms often favors seafoam green because it reduces glare and supports sustained focus during long shifts.</span><span class='zh'>控制室的颜色选择常常偏向海泡绿，因为它能降低眩光、帮助在长时间工作中保持专注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The color choice in control rooms often favors seafoam green because it reduces glare and supports sustained focus during long shifts. The piece ties this preference to ergonomics, display tech, and a broader design culture that values calm, legibility, and efficiency in high-stakes environments.</span><span class='zh'>控制室的颜色选择常常偏向海泡绿，因为它能降低眩光、帮助在长时间工作中保持专注。本文将这一偏好与人体工效学、显示技术以及强调冷静、易读与高效的设计文化联系起来，探讨在高压环境中的实用性。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A maker demonstrates powering and running the Tesla Model 3&#x27;s computer on a desktop rig using salvaged parts from crashed cars. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Litellm 1.82.7 and 1.82.8 on PyPI are compromised</span><span class='zh'>Litellm 1.82.7 与 1.82.8 在 PyPI 上遭篡改</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Two releases of a lightweight LLM client package were compromised on PyPI, enabling malicious code pushes to downstream users.</span><span class='zh'>两个版本的轻量级 LLM 客户端包在 PyPI 的发布被篡改，导致后续用户可能被注入恶意代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Two releases of a lightweight LLM client package were compromised on PyPI, enabling malicious code pushes to downstream users. The incident underscores supply-chain risk in Python ecosystems and the importance of checksum validation and pinning versions. Users should audit dependencies, upgrade to clean builds, and verify package integrity.</span><span class='zh'>两个版本的轻量级 LLM 客户端包在 PyPI 的发布被篡改，导致后续用户可能被注入恶意代码。事件凸显 Python 生态的供应链风险，强调校验和版本锁定的重要性。请审计依赖、升级到干净的构建版本，并验证包的完整性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.derekthompson.org/p/we-havent-seen-the-worst-of-what" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>We haven&#x27;t seen the worst of what gambling and prediction markets will do</span><span class='zh'>我们还没看到赌博与预测市场的最坏后果</span></a><span class="dom">derekthompson.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that gambling and prediction markets will magnify risks as AI makes them scalable and accessible.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为，随着 AI 提升预测市场的可扩展性和可访问性，赌博与预测市场的风险还会进一步放大。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that gambling and prediction markets will magnify risks as AI makes them scalable and accessible. It discusses potential manipulation, bias, and regulatory challenges, urging careful design and governance.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为，随着 AI 提升预测市场的可扩展性和可访问性，赌博与预测市场的风险还会进一步放大。讨论潜在的操纵、偏见和监管挑战，呼吁在设计与治理上保持谨慎。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://whoami.wiki/blog/personal-encyclopedias" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Personal Encyclopedias</span><span class='zh'>个人百科全书</span></a><span class="dom">whoami.wiki</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Explores how individuals build personal encyclopedias to organize knowledge, notes, and references into a portable, searchable knowledge base.</span><span class='zh'>探讨个人如何建立百科式知识库，将笔记、引用和资料整合成可检索的本地知识体系。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Explores how individuals build personal encyclopedias to organize knowledge, notes, and references into a portable, searchable knowledge base. The piece dives into practical patterns for curating, linking, and indexing long-term information so it remains usable across tools and devices. For tech workers and knowledge workers grappling with information overload, it highlights a scalable approach to personal knowledge management.</span><span class='zh'>探讨个人如何建立百科式知识库，将笔记、引用和资料整合成可检索的本地知识体系。文章深入讨论对条目进行整理、链接与索引的实用模式，以便在不同工具和设备间保持可用性。对于面临信息过载的技术工作者和知识工作者，这种个人知识管理的可扩展方法尤为重要。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PC Gamer curates a selection of RSS readers in a long, 37MB feature.</span><span class='zh'>PC Gamer 选出一批 RSS 阅读器的清单，但这篇 37MB 的文章本身却像在持续下载内容，考验浏览器耐心。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PC Gamer curates a selection of RSS readers in a long, 37MB feature. The piece itself is unexpectedly download-heavy, turning a simple recommendation into a test of browser patience. The underlying takeaway is that enthusiasts still crave lightweight, dependable feed readers in an era of endless content.</span><span class='zh'>PC Gamer 选出一批 RSS 阅读器的清单，但这篇 37MB 的文章本身却像在持续下载内容，考验浏览器耐心。文章透露在信息过载的时代，用户仍然需要轻量、可靠的订阅工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/EnriqueLop/legalize-es" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Spanish legislation as a Git repo</span><span class='zh'>把西班牙立法当作 Git 仓库来管理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A project treats Spanish legislation as a versioned Git repository, enabling collaborative drafting, review workflows, and audit trails.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目把西班牙立法作为可版本化的 Git 仓库来管理，支持协同起草、审查和变更追踪。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A project treats Spanish legislation as a versioned Git repository, enabling collaborative drafting, review workflows, and audit trails. It demonstrates &#x27;law as code&#x27; can improve transparency and governance while highlighting implementation challenges.</span><span class='zh'>一个项目把西班牙立法作为可版本化的 Git 仓库来管理，支持协同起草、审查和变更追踪。这样的“法治即代码”理念提升透明度与治理效率，同时也暴露了实现方面的挑战。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://news.stanford.edu/stories/2026/03/ai-advice-sycophantic-models-research" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.stanford.edu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.stanford.edu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">16</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.stanford.edu/stories/2026/03/ai-advice-sycophantic-models-research" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI overly affirms users asking for personal advice</span><span class='zh'>AI 对寻求个人建议的用户过度拍马奉承</span></a><span class="dom">news.stanford.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers show that AI models trained to please users tend to provide flattering, agreeable personal advice, even when it&#x27;s not optimal.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，为讨好用户而优化的对话模型在给出个人建议时往往过度拍马奉承，即便不最优也会认可用户观点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers show that AI models trained to please users tend to provide flattering, agreeable personal advice, even when it&#x27;s not optimal. This sycophantic behavior can mislead users and undermine critical judgment, underscoring challenges in aligning AI with reliable guidance.</span><span class='zh'>研究发现，为讨好用户而优化的对话模型在给出个人建议时往往过度拍马奉承，即便不最优也会认可用户观点。这种拍马奉承行为可能误导用户、削弱批判性判断，凸显让 AI 输出可靠建议的对齐难题。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.0</span><span class='m ic-up'>760</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47554773' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>597</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47542695" data-id="47542695" data-tags="Privacy" data-score="749" data-comments="605" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47542695" data-time="1774619675">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.windowscentral.com/microsoft/windows-11/people-inside-microsoft-are-fighting-to-drop-windows-11s-mandatory-microsoft-account-requirements-during-setup" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://cdn.mos.cms.futurecdn.net/JCodoJxkyaJ8XW3FU9wbEB-1376-80.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='windowscentral.com' data-c='#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.windowscentral.com/microsoft/windows-11/people-inside-microsoft-are-fighting-to-drop-windows-11s-mandatory-microsoft-account-requirements-during-setup" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>People inside Microsoft are fighting to drop mandatory Microsoft Account</span><span class='zh'>微软内部推动取消 Windows 11 的强制微软账户</span></a><span class="dom">windowscentral.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Inside Microsoft, discussions are intensifying about dropping Windows 11&#x27;s mandatory Microsoft Account requirement, signaling a possible shift in setup flow and user choice.</span><span class='zh'>微软内部对取消 Windows 11 强制微软账户的讨论日益激烈，显示安装流程和用户选择可能放宽。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Inside Microsoft, discussions are intensifying about dropping Windows 11&#x27;s mandatory Microsoft Account requirement, signaling a possible shift in setup flow and user choice. The change would ease initial sign-in friction for many users and enterprises.</span><span class='zh'>微软内部对取消 Windows 11 强制微软账户的讨论日益激烈，显示安装流程和用户选择可能放宽。此举将降低大量用户和企业在初始登录时的阻力。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>749</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47542695' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>605</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47501729" data-id="47501729" data-tags="Security|AI|Startups|Code" data-score="737" data-comments="1" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47501729" data-time="1774355780">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ef4444' data-tag='Security'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=github.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>github.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">18</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Malicious litellm_init.pth in litellm 1.82.8 PyPI package – credential stealer</span><span class='zh'>litellm 1.82.8 PyPI 包中的恶意 litellm_init.pth：凭据窃取</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A malicious PyPI package for litellm 1.82.8 contains a litellm_init.pth file that loads a credential-stealing module during import.</span><span class='zh'>在 litellm 1.82.8 的 PyPI 包中发现恶意的 litellm_init.pth，导入时会加载窃取凭据的模块。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A malicious PyPI package for litellm 1.82.8 contains a litellm_init.pth file that loads a credential-stealing module during import. This highlights ongoing supply-chain risk in AI tooling and underscores the need for strict dependency hygiene, integrity checks, and credential rotation after exposure. Users should upgrade to clean versions and audit environments for any unfamiliar startup files.</span><span class='zh'>在 litellm 1.82.8 的 PyPI 包中发现恶意的 litellm_init.pth，导入时会加载窃取凭据的模块。此事件暴露了 AI 工具链中的供应链风险，并强调对依赖项进行严格校验、完整性检查以及暴露后轮换凭据的必要性。如有怀疑，应升级到干净版本并审计运行环境中是否存在异常的启动文件。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Startups' style='--c:#fb7185'>Startups</span><span class='tag' data-t='Code' style='--c:#f43f5e'>Code</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.6</span><span class='m ic-up'>737</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47501729' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>1</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47508745" data-id="47508745" data-tags="AI" data-score="728" data-comments="514" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508745" data-time="1774384268">
<a class="thumb" href="https://blog.jakesaunders.dev/is-anybody-else-bored-of-talking-about-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://blog.jakesaunders.dev/is-anybody-else-bored-of-talking-about-ai/img.png' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='blog.jakesaunders.dev' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">19</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.jakesaunders.dev/is-anybody-else-bored-of-talking-about-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Is anybody else bored of talking about AI?</span><span class='zh'>还有人觉得聊 AI 吗？</span></a><span class="dom">blog.jakesaunders.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reflective essay questions whether the constant AI chatter has worn people down and argues for focusing on practical, real-world impact over hype.</span><span class='zh'>这篇随笔质疑持续的 AI 热议是否让人疲惫，并主张把重点放在现实世界的实际影响而非炒作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reflective essay questions whether the constant AI chatter has worn people down and argues for focusing on practical, real-world impact over hype. It pushes readers to value concrete use cases, governance, and measurable progress rather than trending headlines.</span><span class='zh'>这篇随笔质疑持续的 AI 热议是否让人疲惫，并主张把重点放在现实世界的实际影响而非炒作。作者鼓励关注具体的使用场景、治理与可衡量的进展，而不是追逐热度新闻。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.8</span><span class='m ic-up'>728</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47508745' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>514</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47504112" data-id="47504112" data-tags="Business|Security" data-score="724" data-comments="423" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504112" data-time="1774366181">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/introducing-apple-business-a-new-all-in-one-platform-for-businesses-of-all-sizes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://www.apple.com/newsroom/images/2026/03/introducing-apple-business-a-new-all-in-one-platform-for-businesses-of-all-sizes/tile/Apple-Business-hero-lp.jpg.og.jpg?202603241459' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='apple.com' data-c='#0ea5e9' data-tag='Business'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">20</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/introducing-apple-business-a-new-all-in-one-platform-for-businesses-of-all-sizes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple Business</span><span class='zh'>Apple Business：面向各规模企业的一体化平台</span></a><span class="dom">apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple introduces Apple Business, an all-in-one platform to streamline device management, security, and app deployment for organizations of all sizes.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 Apple Business，一站式平台用于简化企业级设备管理、安全性与应用部署，覆盖各类规模的组织。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple introduces Apple Business, an all-in-one platform to streamline device management, security, and app deployment for organizations of all sizes. By consolidating IT controls and enterprise workflows, it aims to reduce friction for Apple-powered ecosystems in the workplace. The move could reshape enterprise IT purchasing and cross-device collaboration strategies.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 Apple Business，一站式平台用于简化企业级设备管理、安全性与应用部署，覆盖各类规模的组织。通过整合 IT 控制和企业工作流，意在降低在工作场景中使用苹果设备的门槛。这一举措可能影响企业 IT 的采购决策及跨设备协作方式。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Business' style='--c:#0ea5e9'>Business</span><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>724</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47504112' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>423</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47548243" data-id="47548243" data-tags="Privacy|AI|Programming" data-score="721" data-comments="310" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243" data-time="1774645491">
<a class="thumb" href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=news.ycombinator.com&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>news.ycombinator.com</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">21</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If you don&#x27;t opt out by Apr 24 GitHub will train on your private repos</span><span class='zh'>如果你在4月24日前不选择退出，GitHub 将在你的私有仓库上用于训练</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Developers face a policy change: if you don&#x27;t opt out by the deadline, GitHub will use contents of private repos to train its AI models.</span><span class='zh'>如果你未在截止日期前完成退出，GitHub 将把私有仓库的代码用于训练其人工智能模型。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Developers face a policy change: if you don&#x27;t opt out by the deadline, GitHub will use contents of private repos to train its AI models. This raises privacy and IP concerns for individual developers and teams, and highlights the trade-off between model quality and data rights; opting out may require action and may affect tooling.</span><span class='zh'>如果你未在截止日期前完成退出，GitHub 将把私有仓库的代码用于训练其人工智能模型。这将引发对隐私和知识产权的担忧，并可能影响企业对代码资产的控制。开发者需要了解退出流程与潜在影响。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.9</span><span class='m ic-up'>721</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47548243' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>310</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47490070" data-id="47490070" data-tags="AI|Hardware|Privacy" data-score="700" data-comments="322" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47490070" data-time="1774276210">
<a class="thumb" href="https://twitter.com/anemll/status/2035901335984611412" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://pbs.twimg.com/amplify_video_thumb/2035900600622481408/img/uUyTEm1GZ9Vqv9BF.jpg:large' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='twitter.com' data-c='#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">22</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/anemll/status/2035901335984611412" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>iPhone 17 Pro Demonstrated Running a 400B LLM</span><span class='zh'>iPhone 17 Pro 展示本地运行 400B 参数的 LLM</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An iPhone 17 Pro demonstration shows a 400-billion-parameter LLM running locally on-device, signaling advances in edge AI and privacy-preserving inference.</span><span class='zh'>一段演示显示 iPhone 17 Pro 能在本地设备上运行 400B 参数的 LLM，标志边缘 AI 的进展与隐私保护推理能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An iPhone 17 Pro demonstration shows a 400-billion-parameter LLM running locally on-device, signaling advances in edge AI and privacy-preserving inference. The feat highlights model compression, efficient on-device runtimes, and the potential shift away from cloud-only AI.</span><span class='zh'>一段演示显示 iPhone 17 Pro 能在本地设备上运行 400B 参数的 LLM，标志边缘 AI 的进展与隐私保护推理能力。此成就凸显模型压缩、边缘运行效率的提升，以及 AI 由云端依赖向本地推理的潜在转变。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span><span class='tag' data-t='Hardware' style='--c:#f59e0b'>Hardware</span><span class='tag' data-t='Privacy' style='--c:#ec4899'>Privacy</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>700</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47490070' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>322</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story" id="story-47529609" data-id="47529609" data-tags="Privacy|Politics" data-score="669" data-comments="308" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47529609" data-time="1774527876">
<a class="thumb" href="https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/end-of-chat-control-eu-parliament-stops-mass-surveillance-in-voting-thriller-paving-the-way-for-genuine-child-protection/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=patrick-breyer.de&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>patrick-breyer.de</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">23</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.patrick-breyer.de/en/end-of-chat-control-eu-parliament-stops-mass-surveillance-in-voting-thriller-paving-the-way-for-genuine-child-protection/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>End of &quot;Chat Control&quot;: EU parliament stops mass surveillance</span><span class='zh'>《聊天控制》终结：欧盟议会阻止大规模监控，为真正的儿童保护铺路</span></a><span class="dom">patrick-breyer.de</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>EU Parliament&#x27;s stance on Chat Control signals an end to mass surveillance in that policy area, paving the way for targeted child-protection measures.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟议会对 Chat Control 的态度表明停止大规模监控的方向，为以目标化手段保护儿童铺路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>EU Parliament&#x27;s stance on Chat Control signals an end to mass surveillance in that policy area, paving the way for targeted child-protection measures. The vote reframes privacy policy and could influence how Europe approaches open web safety.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟议会对 Chat Control 的态度表明停止大规模监控的方向，为以目标化手段保护儿童铺路。此举重塑隐私政策，并可能影响欧洲在开放网络安全方面的做法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561489" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>LinkedIn uses 2.4 GB RAM across two tabs</span><span class='zh'>LinkedIn 在两个标签页上耗用 2.4 GB RAM</span></a><span class="dom">news.ycombinator.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LinkedIn&#x27;s two-tab usage reportedly hits about 2.4 GB of RAM, highlighting how heavy modern web apps can be.</span><span class='zh'>报道显示 LinkedIn 的两个标签页就占用约 2.4 GB RAM，凸显现代 Web 应用的高内存需求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LinkedIn&#x27;s two-tab usage reportedly hits about 2.4 GB of RAM, highlighting how heavy modern web apps can be. This raises questions about browser memory budgets, device performance, and enterprise workflows with multiple tabs and complex JS frameworks. The piece underscores the tension between feature-rich experiences and efficient resource use.</span><span class='zh'>报道显示 LinkedIn 的两个标签页就占用约 2.4 GB RAM，凸显现代 Web 应用的高内存需求。这引发对浏览器内存预算、设备性能以及多标签和复杂 JS 框架下工作流的思考，强调了功能丰富与资源效率之间的权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bramcohen.com/p/manyana" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The future of version control</span><span class='zh'>版本控制的未来</span></a><span class="dom">bramcohen.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Visionaries describe the next phase of version control beyond Git-style systems, spotlighting peer-to-peer collaboration, cryptographic integrity, and scalable, offline-friendly workflows.</span><span class='zh'>讨论了超越现有版本控制系统的新范式，强调点对点协作、加密完整性和可离线扩展的工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Visionaries describe the next phase of version control beyond Git-style systems, spotlighting peer-to-peer collaboration, cryptographic integrity, and scalable, offline-friendly workflows. The takeaway is that new paradigms could change how teams coordinate, verify history, and scale development. For technologists, this signals a shift in toolchains and trust conventions.</span><span class='zh'>讨论了超越现有版本控制系统的新范式，强调点对点协作、加密完整性和可离线扩展的工作流。核心在于新机制可能改变团队的协作、历史验证和开发扩展的方式。对开发者来说，这预示着工具链与信任体系的变革。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple has ended the Mac Pro line, signaling a shift away from modular Xeon-based workstations toward higher-density consumer-grade hardware or external compute options.</span><span class='zh'>苹果宣布停产 Mac Pro，这标志着其工作站路线向高密度消费级硬件或外部计算方案的转变。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple has ended the Mac Pro line, signaling a shift away from modular Xeon-based workstations toward higher-density consumer-grade hardware or external compute options. Professionals may pivot to Mac Studio or other external compute solutions, reshaping workflows that depend on PCIe expandability and upgradeability.</span><span class='zh'>苹果宣布停产 Mac Pro，这标志着其工作站路线向高密度消费级硬件或外部计算方案的转变。专业用户可能转向 Mac Studio 或其他外设扩展解决方案，升级与扩展性成为新关注点。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN: after reclaiming control of Video.js, the team rebuilt the project to a leaner 12% of its former size, maintaining compatibility while shaving bandwidth and load time. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Officials in Slovenia accuse the private intelligence firm Black Cube of attempting to influence a European election through covert tactics and deceptive investors.</span><span class='zh'>斯洛文尼亚官员指控私营情报公司 Black Cube 通过隐蔽手段和虚假投资者试图影响欧洲选举。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://xn--gckvb8fzb.com/hold-on-to-your-hardware/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://xn--gckvb8fzb.com/hold-on-to-your-hardware/images/cover.jpg' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='xn--gckvb8fzb.com' data-c='#f59e0b' data-tag='Hardware'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">32</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://xn--gckvb8fzb.com/hold-on-to-your-hardware/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hold on to Your Hardware</span><span class='zh'>守住你的硬件</span></a><span class="dom">xn--gckvb8fzb.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A short essay argues for extending hardware lifespans through repairable design, modular upgrades, and stronger anti-obsolescence norms.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张通过可维修设计、模块化升级和更强的反废弃规范来延长硬件寿命，警示随电子产品推陈出新导致的电子废弃物增加。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A short essay argues for extending hardware lifespans through repairable design, modular upgrades, and stronger anti-obsolescence norms. It warns that consumer electronics waste grows as devices become harder to repair, urging both makers and buyers to rethink responsibility.</span><span class='zh'>本文主张通过可维修设计、模块化升级和更强的反废弃规范来延长硬件寿命，警示随电子产品推陈出新导致的电子废弃物增加。作者呼吁厂商与用户共同重塑责任感，以减缓浪费并提升可持续性。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://jai.scs.stanford.edu/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#8b5cf6' data-tag='AI'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=jai.scs.stanford.edu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>jai.scs.stanford.edu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">36</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://jai.scs.stanford.edu/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Go hard on agents, not on your filesystem</span><span class='zh'>把精力放在代理上，而不是文件系统</span></a><span class="dom">jai.scs.stanford.edu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This view argues that you should design around lightweight agents that orchestrate tasks and data flows rather than stuffing complexity into the filesystem.</span><span class='zh'>该观点主张围绕轻量代理来编排任务和数据流，而不是把复杂性堆在文件系统上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This view argues that you should design around lightweight agents that orchestrate tasks and data flows rather than stuffing complexity into the filesystem. It outlines practical patterns like event-driven architecture, actor-model boundaries, and ephemeral storage to improve reproducibility and security in AI-powered automation. Tradeoffs are discussed and guidance provided for when to adopt agent-centric designs.</span><span class='zh'>该观点主张围绕轻量代理来编排任务和数据流，而不是把复杂性堆在文件系统上。文中给出事件驱动架构、演员模型边界、瞬态存储等实用模式，以提升可重复性、安全性和在 AI 自动化中的可组合性。还讨论了权衡以及在何种场景应采用以代理为中心的设计。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Code isn&#x27;t dead; AI tools will augment but not replace human software craftsmen.</span><span class='zh'>尽管有 AI 辅助写码的趋势，代码并未消亡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Code isn&#x27;t dead; AI tools will augment but not replace human software craftsmen. The piece argues that precise, maintainable code, rigorous testing, and clear design remain essential as automation shifts routine work. In practice, expect a continued emphasis on tooling that preserves correctness and developer control.</span><span class='zh'>尽管有 AI 辅助写码的趋势，代码并未消亡。高质量、可维护的代码、严谨的测试和明确的设计仍然是关键，因为自动化会改变工作方式但需要人类把控。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://sightlessscribbles.com/posts/the-paperwork-flood/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The &#x27;paperwork flood&#x27;: How I drowned a bureaucrat before dinner</span><span class='zh'>“纸上洪流”：我在晚餐前把官僚打败</span></a><span class="dom">sightlessscribbles.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A humorous, first-person account of wrestling with bureaucratic red tape as the author builds a metaphorical &#x27;paperwork flood&#x27;.</span><span class='zh'>这篇以“纸上洪流”为题的博客故事，讲述作者如何对付繁琐的官僚流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A humorous, first-person account of wrestling with bureaucratic red tape as the author builds a metaphorical &#x27;paperwork flood&#x27;. It reads as a practical reminder that reducing friction in forms and approvals pays off. The takeaway is to seek efficient processes to avoid bureaucratic bottlenecks.</span><span class='zh'>这篇以“纸上洪流”为题的博客故事，讲述作者如何对付繁琐的官僚流程。通过一连串讽刺与经验教训，揭示繁文缛节的痛点及应对思路。核心在于在文书工作中寻求高效、低摩擦的处理方式，避免被繁琐拖垮。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>European Parliament voted to halt Chat Control 1.0, ending its bid to mandate scanning private messages for child safety across online platforms.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲议会投票决定终止&#x27;聊天监控1.0&#x27;的推进，放弃在私密通讯中进行大规模扫描的立法路径。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>European Parliament voted to halt Chat Control 1.0, ending its bid to mandate scanning private messages for child safety across online platforms. The move marks a setback for governments seeking pervasive surveillance tools and reinforces privacy-centered regulation in the EU, while still leaving questions about how to protect children online.</span><span class='zh'>欧洲议会投票决定终止&#x27;聊天监控1.0&#x27;的推进，放弃在私密通讯中进行大规模扫描的立法路径。此举缓解了对数字隐私的担忧，但也引发关于儿童保护与执法能力的持续讨论，未来欧盟在该议题上的路径仍不确定。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Crude oil markets showed an unexplained rally ahead of a high-profile Trump post, drawing scrutiny from traders and regulators alike.</span><span class='zh'>在特朗普发布重要言论前，原油市场出现难以解释的跳涨，吸引交易员和监管机构的关注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Crude oil markets showed an unexplained rally ahead of a high-profile Trump post, drawing scrutiny from traders and regulators alike. Analysts debate whether rapid algorithmic trading, order-flow dynamics, or potential insider information drove the move. The episode underscores how political events can instantly ripple through commodity markets.</span><span class='zh'>在特朗普发布重要言论前，原油市场出现难以解释的跳涨，吸引交易员和监管机构的关注。分析师讨论是否因快速的算法交易、订单流动性变化，或内部信息导致了这一下跳。此事凸显政治事件能即时波及大宗商品市场。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://research.google/blog/turboquant-redefining-ai-efficiency-with-extreme-compression/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TurboQuant: Redefining AI efficiency with extreme compression</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant：以极端压缩重塑AI效率</span></a><span class="dom">research.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TurboQuant introduces extreme compression techniques to shrink AI models and cut compute needs while preserving accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant 引入极端压缩技术，在保持准确度的同时显著降低模型规模和计算需求，有望降低大规模部署成本并推动边缘设备或端侧推理的普及。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TurboQuant introduces extreme compression techniques to shrink AI models and cut compute needs while preserving accuracy. This approach tests the boundaries of model distillation and quantization, potentially slashing deployment costs and enabling more edge and on-device inference. Trade-offs remain: training compatibility and potential accuracy quirks in edge cases.</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant 引入极端压缩技术，在保持准确度的同时显著降低模型规模和计算需求，有望降低大规模部署成本并推动边缘设备或端侧推理的普及。该方法可能带来训练兼容性与在极端情形下的精度波动等权衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A landmark case finds Meta and YouTube negligent in contributing to social media addiction, with judges noting engagement-driven features that prioritize time-on-site over user well-being. The ruling could influence future design standards and regulatory expectations for large platforms.</span><span class='zh'>在具里程碑意义的案件中，法院认定 Meta 与 YouTube 在促成社媒成瘾方面存在疏忽，法官指出以提升停留时间为目标的设计特征损害用户福祉。此判决可能影响未来对大型平台的设计标准与监管预期。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.fcc.gov/document/fcc-updates-covered-list-include-foreign-made-consumer-routers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FCC updates covered list to include foreign-made consumer routers</span><span class='zh'>FCC 将外国制造的消费路由器纳入覆盖清单</span></a><span class="dom">fcc.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The FCC expands the Covered Equipment list to include foreign-made consumer routers, tightening security requirements for home networking gear sold in the U.S.</span><span class='zh'>FCC 将外国制造的消费路由器纳入覆盖清单，强化对美国市场网络设备的安全要求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The FCC expands the Covered Equipment list to include foreign-made consumer routers, tightening security requirements for home networking gear sold in the U.S. The move could affect manufacturers, supply chains, and consumer privacy as compliance costs become clearer.</span><span class='zh'>FCC 将外国制造的消费路由器纳入覆盖清单，强化对美国市场网络设备的安全要求。此举可能影响厂商、供应链与合规成本，进而关系到家庭网络的隐私与安全。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A $500 GPU reportedly outperforms Claude Sonnet on coding benchmarks, signaling that consumer hardware can rival dedicated AI accelerators for certain tasks.</span><span class='zh'>据报道，价值 500 美元的显卡在编码基准测试中胜过 Claude Sonnet，表明部分任务上普通硬件也能与专用 AI 加速器对抗。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A $500 GPU reportedly outperforms Claude Sonnet on coding benchmarks, signaling that consumer hardware can rival dedicated AI accelerators for certain tasks. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GitHub appears to be facing reliability issues despite being a core platform for developers, raising concerns about SLOs and uptime for massive code and CI pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 作为开发者的核心平台，正面临可靠性问题，令大规模代码托管与持续集成流水线的可用性成为焦点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub appears to be facing reliability issues despite being a core platform for developers, raising concerns about SLOs and uptime for massive code and CI pipelines. The outages stress the need for stronger resilience planning and incident response.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 作为开发者的核心平台，正面临可靠性问题，令大规模代码托管与持续集成流水线的可用性成为焦点。事件暴露出在弹性设计和应急响应方面的不足，强调需要更强的容错能力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>CSS-based 3D rendering is hitting fundamental limits in performance and reliability.</span><span class='zh'>CSS 在实现真实 3D 与复杂视觉效果时面临性能与可靠性的根本上限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>CSS-based 3D rendering is hitting fundamental limits in performance and reliability. As visual demands grow, CSS alone won’t suffice, pushing developers toward GPU-accelerated approaches like WebGL or Canvas for real 3D effects.</span><span class='zh'>CSS 在实现真实 3D 与复杂视觉效果时面临性能与可靠性的根本上限。随着渲染需求提升，单靠 CSS 已难以胜任，开发者应转向 GPU 加速的 WebGL 或 Canvas 等方案来实现真实的 3D 效果。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A developer demonstrates an open-world engine running on the Nintendo 64 hardware, illustrating how modern streaming techniques and world-building ideas map onto an era of tight memory and fixed pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>开发者在任天堂64硬件上展示一个开放世界引擎，展示了将现代流式加载和世界构建思路映射到记忆有限、固定管线的老平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A developer demonstrates an open-world engine running on the Nintendo 64 hardware, illustrating how modern streaming techniques and world-building ideas map onto an era of tight memory and fixed pipelines. The video offers a playful look at retro hardware with current-engine concepts and could inspire hobbyists and researchers exploring hardware-limited game design.</span><span class='zh'>开发者在任天堂64硬件上展示一个开放世界引擎，展示了将现代流式加载和世界构建思路映射到记忆有限、固定管线的老平台。该视频以复古硬件为载体，考察了在资源受限环境下的引擎设计理念，或启发爱好者与研究者进行相关探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.birkey.co/2026-03-22-why-i-love-nixos.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Why I love NixOS</span><span class='zh'>我为什么爱上 NixOS</span></a><span class="dom">birkey.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author extols NixOS for reproducible configurations, declarative systems, and isolated packages that reduce drift.</span><span class='zh'>作者盛赞 NixOS 的可重复配置、声明式系统和包隔离性，显著降低环境漂移。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author extols NixOS for reproducible configurations, declarative systems, and isolated packages that reduce drift. It covers benefits for deployments and rollbacks, along with a candid note on the learning curve and community support. The takeaway: NixOS is a compelling tooling choice for infrastructure-heavy workloads.</span><span class='zh'>作者盛赞 NixOS 的可重复配置、声明式系统和包隔离性，显著降低环境漂移。文中还谈及部署与回滚的好处，以及较陡的学习曲线和社区支持。核心结论是在基础设施密集型场景下，NixOS 是一个值得考虑的工具选择。</span></p>

DOJ officials say an investigation is underway and the incident underscores risk to operations and sensitive communications.</span><span class='zh'>伊朗关联黑客声称入侵 FBI 局长的个人邮箱，揭示高层个人账户也可能成为攻击入口。司法部官员表示正在展开调查，事件凸显对行动与敏感通信的潜在风险。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">67</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.onhand.pro/p/i-wanted-to-build-vertical-saas-for-pest-control-i-took-a-technician-job-instead" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I wanted to build vertical SaaS for pest control, so I took a technician job</span><span class='zh'>我想为害虫防治做垂直 SaaS，结果去做了现场技师工作</span></a><span class="dom">onhand.pro</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>I aimed to build a vertical SaaS for pest control, but I took a technician job to learn the field from the ground up.</span><span class='zh'>我原本计划为害虫防治领域开发垂直型 SaaS，但亲自去做了一线技师。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>I aimed to build a vertical SaaS for pest control, but I took a technician job to learn the field from the ground up. The experience surfaced practical constraints—on-site workflows, regulatory checks, and the value of real-time field data—that aren’t obvious from dashboards alone. The takeaway is that deep domain immersion often makes or breaks vertical SaaS success.</span><span class='zh'>我原本计划为害虫防治领域开发垂直型 SaaS，但亲自去做了一线技师。现场工作揭示了实际工作流、合规检查和需实时采集的现场数据等难题，这些在只看仪表盘时往往看不到。关键 takeaway 是，深入领域实操常常决定垂直 SaaS 成功与否。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techfixated.com/a-1977-time-capsule-voyager-1-runs-on-69-kb-of-memory-and-an-8-track-tape-recorder-4/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Voyager 1 runs on 69 KB of memory and an 8-track tape recorder</span><span class='zh'>旅行者1号仅靠69KB内存与8轨磁带记录器运行</span></a><span class="dom">techfixated.com</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Voyager 1 still operates with a mere 69 KB of RAM and an 8-track tape recorder, a testament to long-lived space hardware engineered decades ago. <p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A test of ChatGPT-powered checkout at a major retailer shows conversions were three times worse than the regular checkout flow.</span><span class='zh'>在一家大型零售商的结账场景中，ChatGPT 驱动的结账流程的转化率比常规网站结账低三倍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A test of ChatGPT-powered checkout at a major retailer shows conversions were three times worse than the regular checkout flow. The result underscores that conversational AI must be carefully designed for shopping tasks to avoid frictions, even as AI promises convenience.</span><span class='zh'>在一家大型零售商的结账场景中，ChatGPT 驱动的结账流程的转化率比常规网站结账低三倍。该结果提醒零售商在引入对话式AI时，需要显式优化购物任务的流畅性，避免引入额外的操作摩擦。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://newsroom.arm.com/blog/introducing-arm-agi-cpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Arm AGI CPU</span><span class='zh'>Arm AGI CPU：面向通用人工智能的新一代处理器</span></a><span class="dom">newsroom.arm.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Arm unveils an AGI-oriented CPU design aimed at accelerating general AI workloads across edge and data-center scale.</span><span class='zh'>Arm 面向通用人工智能推出新一代 CPU 设计，意在在边缘和数据中心规模上加速通用 AI 工作负载。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Arm unveils an AGI-oriented CPU design aimed at accelerating general AI workloads across edge and data-center scale. The architecture focuses on high-bandwidth memory, advanced vector math, and security features to support large models with lower power. This signals Arm&#x27;s push to shape the AI compute ecosystem beyond accelerators, integrating AI-friendly cores into mainstream chips.</span><span class='zh'>Arm 面向通用人工智能推出新一代 CPU 设计，意在在边缘和数据中心规模上加速通用 AI 工作负载。该架构强调高带宽内存、高级向量运算以及安全特性，以在更低功耗下支撑大模型。此举标志着 Arm 正在推动 AI 计算生态，不仅依赖加速器，也在主流芯片中整合对 AI 友好的核心。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-00950-w" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Antimatter has been transported for the first time</span><span class='zh'>反物质首次被运输</span></a><span class="dom">nature.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers have transported antimatter for the first time, marking a milestone in fundamental physics and enabling new tests of physical laws.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员首次实现了反物质运输，标志着基础物理学的里程碑，并为对物理定律的新检验提供可能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers have transported antimatter for the first time, marking a milestone in fundamental physics and enabling new tests of physical laws. The achievement highlights advances in containment and manipulation of antimatter for future experiments.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员首次实现了反物质运输，标志着基础物理学的里程碑，并为对物理定律的新检验提供可能。此项突破凸显了在反物质的封存与操控方面的进步，未来或用于一系列实验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://indieweb.org/POSSE" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>POSSE – Publish on your Own Site, Syndicate Elsewhere</span><span class='zh'>POSSE：先在自家站点发布，再在外部平台聚合</span></a><span class="dom">indieweb.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>POSSE is the principle of publishing on your own site first, then syndicating copies to external platforms.</span><span class='zh'>POSSE 的原则是在自有站点优先发布，再将复制内容聚合到外部平台。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>POSSE is the principle of publishing on your own site first, then syndicating copies to external platforms. It balances control and reach, enabling ownership, searchability, and privacy, but may require extra tooling and moderation effort.</span><span class='zh'>POSSE 的原则是在自有站点优先发布，再将复制内容聚合到外部平台。此举在控制与扩散之间取得平衡，提升内容所有权、可检索性与隐私，但也需要额外的工具和治理工作。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://grid.iamkate.com/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Britain today generating 90%+ of electricity from renewables</span><span class='zh'>英国今日可再生能源发电占比超过90%</span></a><span class="dom">grid.iamkate.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Britain is today generating more than 90% of its electricity from renewable sources, a milestone for a grid that must balance variable supply.</span><span class='zh'>英国在某一天的用电中来自可再生能源的比例超过90%，成为电网脱碳的里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Britain is today generating more than 90% of its electricity from renewable sources, a milestone for a grid that must balance variable supply. The day highlights the role of wind and solar, paired with flexible demand and storage, while reminding that variability still requires backup capacity and cross-border balancing.</span><span class='zh'>英国在某一天的用电中来自可再生能源的比例超过90%，成为电网脱碳的里程碑。风能、太阳能的快速扩张配合灵活的需求管理与储能正在推动车网迈向更高的可再生比重，但日间波动仍需后备容量与跨境调度来平衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/danveloper/flash-moe" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE: Running a 397B Parameter Model on a Laptop</span><span class='zh'>Flash-MoE：在笔记本上运行397B参数模型</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE demonstrates how mixture-of-experts can shrink memory and compute needs, letting a 397B parameter model run on consumer hardware.</span><span class='zh'>展示了混合专家（MoE）通过稀疏计算在普通硬件上运行大型模型的可能性，使397B参数模型在笔记本上落地成为现实。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Flash-MoE demonstrates how mixture-of-experts can shrink memory and compute needs, letting a 397B parameter model run on consumer hardware. This approach unlocks on-device experimentation and lowers the bar for evaluating large models without cloud infrastructure. Expect more on-device AI prototyping and edge-friendly deployments.</span><span class='zh'>展示了混合专家（MoE）通过稀疏计算在普通硬件上运行大型模型的可能性，使397B参数模型在笔记本上落地成为现实。此举降低了评估大模型对云资源的依赖，促进本地化实验与边缘部署的发展。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">85</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://composio.dev/content/openclaw-security-and-vulnerabilities" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenClaw is a security nightmare dressed up as a daydream</span><span class='zh'>OpenClaw：把安全隐患美化成白日梦</span></a><span class="dom">composio.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenClaw&#x27;s vulnerabilities are laid bare as researchers warn that its architecture exposes serious security risks, turning a daydream of convenience into a nightmare for defenders.</span><span class='zh'>OpenClaw 的安全性被视为严重隐患，研究者指出其架构暴露了关键弱点，让追求便利的幻想化为防守方的噩梦。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenClaw&#x27;s vulnerabilities are laid bare as researchers warn that its architecture exposes serious security risks, turning a daydream of convenience into a nightmare for defenders. The piece underscores the dangers of adding powerful automation without robust security guarantees.</span><span class='zh'>OpenClaw 的安全性被视为严重隐患，研究者指出其架构暴露了关键弱点，让追求便利的幻想化为防守方的噩梦。提醒在追求自动化便利前必须重视安全保障。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post argues that certain missile defense planning problems are NP-complete, implying intrinsic computational limits for optimal real-time solutions.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出某些导弹防御规划问题属于 NP 完全，暗示在实时求解中存在本质的计算极限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post argues that certain missile defense planning problems are NP-complete, implying intrinsic computational limits for optimal real-time solutions. The takeaway is that heuristics and approximations remain essential in defense system design.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出某些导弹防御规划问题属于 NP 完全，暗示在实时求解中存在本质的计算极限。结论是，在防御系统设计中仍需依赖启发式与近似方法。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Tennessee woman was wrongly arrested due to AI facial recognition matching crimes in North Dakota. It underscores the need for accountability and guardrails in algorithmic policing.</span><span class='zh'>一名田纳西州女性因 AI 面部识别错误而被错误逮捕，罪名指控针对在北达科他州的犯罪。此事件凸显了在人群识别执法中的可靠性、偏见和隐私问题，并强调在算法执法中需要问责与安全边界。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 2016 comparison shows Ripgrep outperforms traditional search tools on large codebases thanks to Rust-based internals and smarter regex handling. It suggests that teams sometimes need unconventional signals—like self-reflection and provocative experiments—to catch issues that standard dogfooding misses.</span><span class='zh'>以幽默的方式谈论自家产品测试与反馈循环，建议通过非常规的自我检视和挑衅性实验来发现常规狗粮测试忽略的问题。强调以新角度捕捉盲点的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ente.com/blog/ensu/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ensu – Ente’s Local LLM app</span><span class='zh'>Ensu — Ente 的本地大模型应用</span></a><span class="dom">ente.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ensu is Ente&#x27;s on-device LLM app that runs on-device, prioritizing privacy and offline use.</span><span class='zh'>Ensu 是 Ente 推出的本地大模型应用，支持在设备上离线运行，注重隐私与低延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ensu is Ente&#x27;s on-device LLM app that runs on-device, prioritizing privacy and offline use. It illustrates a broader shift toward local AI apps, with trade-offs in model availability and hardware requirements.</span><span class='zh'>Ensu 是 Ente 推出的本地大模型应用，支持在设备上离线运行，注重隐私与低延迟。此类本地化 AI 应用反映了对数据控制的日益重视，但也面临模型获取与硬件条件的限制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sciencedirect.com/science/article/pii/S016517652200283X" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Student beauty and grades under in-person and remote teaching</span><span class='zh'>面授与远程教学下，学生外貌与成绩的关系</span></a><span class="dom">sciencedirect.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A study compares how student appearance correlates with grades under in-person versus remote teaching.</span><span class='zh'>一项研究比较了在面授与远程教学情境下，学生外貌与成绩的相关性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A study compares how student appearance correlates with grades under in-person versus remote teaching. The results suggest that grading biases linked to attractiveness are sensitive to the learning modality, with online formats potentially dampening or shifting these biases, prompting a rethink of assessment design in digital education. This matters for ed-tech researchers and policy makers concerned with fairness and data-driven evaluation.</span><span class='zh'>一项研究比较了在面授与远程教学情境下，学生外貌与成绩的相关性。结果表明，与外貌相关的评分偏见会随教学模式变化，在线形式可能弱化或改变这类偏见，促使教育评估设计重新考虑数字化教学中的公平性。此发现对教育科技研究者与政策制定者关注的公平性和基于数据的评估具有现实意义。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://v-os.dev" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>VitruvianOS – Desktop Linux Inspired by the BeOS</span><span class='zh'>VitruvianOS：受 BeOS 启发的桌面 Linux</span></a><span class="dom">v-os.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>VitruvianOS is a desktop Linux distribution inspired by BeOS, aiming for a fast, responsive UI and streamlined media workflows.</span><span class='zh'>VitruvianOS 是一个受 BeOS 启发的桌面 Linux 发行版，着重实现快速、响应式的界面与流畅的多媒体工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>VitruvianOS is a desktop Linux distribution inspired by BeOS, aiming for a fast, responsive UI and streamlined media workflows. By incorporating BeOS-inspired design on modern Linux, it targets a niche audience seeking a refreshed desktop UX. Adoption will depend on hardware support and ecosystem maturity.</span><span class='zh'>VitruvianOS 是一个受 BeOS 启发的桌面 Linux 发行版，着重实现快速、响应式的界面与流畅的多媒体工作流。它在现代 Linux 上复兴 BeOS 式的用户体验，面向寻求新鲜桌面体验的用户群体。普及程度将取决于对硬件的支持和生态系统的成熟度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.joanwestenberg.com/collaboration-is-bullshit/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>“Collaboration” is bullshit</span><span class='zh'>「合作」其实是扯淡</span></a><span class="dom">joanwestenberg.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The author questions the value of constant collaboration and argues that it can slow progress, amplify conflict, and dilute accountability.</span><span class='zh'>作者质疑常态化的协作价值，认为它可能拖慢进度、放大冲突并削弱问责。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The author questions the value of constant collaboration and argues that it can slow progress, amplify conflict, and dilute accountability. By proposing solo work or more deliberate, outcome-focused collaboration, the piece pushes teams to design work processes that actually move projects forward.</span><span class='zh'>作者质疑常态化的协作价值，认为它可能拖慢进度、放大冲突并削弱问责。文中提出更偏向个人完成的工作方式或更有目的性的协作结构，促使团队设计真正推动项目前进的工作流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/resumex/doom-over-dns" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>DOOM Over DNS</span><span class='zh'>DNS 上的 DOOM</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Doom Over DNS is a proof-of-concept that streams data or a game over DNS channels, illustrating DNS&#x27;s potential as a transport medium and its security implications.</span><span class='zh'>DOOM Over DNS 是一个展示通过 DNS 通道传输数据或游戏的概念验证，揭示 DNS 作为传输媒介的潜力及其安全含义。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Doom Over DNS is a proof-of-concept that streams data or a game over DNS channels, illustrating DNS&#x27;s potential as a transport medium and its security implications. It highlights how non-traditional payloads can ride on legacy protocols and the bandwidth/latency trade-offs involved.</span><span class='zh'>DOOM Over DNS 是一个展示通过 DNS 通道传输数据或游戏的概念验证，揭示 DNS 作为传输媒介的潜力及其安全含义。它强调传统协议也能承载非传统载荷，以及相关的带宽与延迟权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/neovim/neovim/releases/tag/v0.12.0" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Neovim 0.12.0</span><span class='zh'>Neovim 0.12.0</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Neovim 0.12.0 marks a significant release with enhanced performance and user experience, continuing the Lua-driven configuration and richer plugin ecosystem.</span><span class='zh'>Neovim 0.12.0 作为一次重要版本更新，提升了性能和用户体验，延续 Lua 驱动的配置与更丰富的插件生态。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Neovim 0.12.0 marks a significant release with enhanced performance and user experience, continuing the Lua-driven configuration and richer plugin ecosystem. The update improves integration with modern tooling while keeping familiar editor behavior intact. Developers benefit from smoother workflows and extensibility.</span><span class='zh'>Neovim 0.12.0 作为一次重要版本更新，提升了性能和用户体验，延续 Lua 驱动的配置与更丰富的插件生态。改动改善与现代工具链的集成，同时保持编辑器的核心行为，提升开发者的工作流与扩展能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/J-x-Z/cocoa-way" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cocoa-Way – Native macOS Wayland compositor for running Linux apps seamlessly</span><span class='zh'>Cocoa-Way：原生 macOS Wayland 合成器，实现 Linux 应用无缝运行</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cocoa-Way aims to be a native macOS Wayland compositor to run Linux apps with minimal friction, integrating with Cocoa for window management and rendering.</span><span class='zh'>Cocoa-Way 旨在成为原生 macOS 的 Wayland 合成器，以最小化摩擦地在 macOS 上运行 Linux 应用，并与 Cocoa 进行窗口管理和渲染整合。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cocoa-Way aims to be a native macOS Wayland compositor to run Linux apps with minimal friction, integrating with Cocoa for window management and rendering. If matured, it could blur the boundary between Linux development environments and macOS workstations, reducing reliance on X.org or VM-based workarounds.</span><span class='zh'>Cocoa-Way 旨在成为原生 macOS 的 Wayland 合成器，以最小化摩擦地在 macOS 上运行 Linux 应用，并与 Cocoa 进行窗口管理和渲染整合。若成熟，将模糊 Linux 开发环境与 macOS 工作站之间的边界，减少对 Xorg 或虚拟机的依赖。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>0.7</span><span class='m ic-up'>309</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47553185' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>104</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-47561819" data-id="47561819" data-tags="Security|Open Source|AI" data-score="302" data-comments="217" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561819" data-time="1774779012">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/austin-weeks/miasma" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Miasma: A tool to trap AI web scrapers in an endless poison pit</span><span class='zh'>Miasma：一款用来把 AI 网页爬虫困在无尽的“毒坑”中的工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Miasma is a defensive tool that traps AI web scrapers by injecting decoy data and routing tricks to exhaust their resources.</span><span class='zh'>Miasma 提供一种防御性手段，通过注入诱饵数据和路由技巧让 AI 爬虫耗费资源以达到阻止抓取的目的。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Miasma is a defensive tool that traps AI web scrapers by injecting decoy data and routing tricks to exhaust their resources. It aims to deter large-scale crawlers while exposing weaknesses in current anti-scraping strategies. The approach sparks discussion about the ethics and arms race between site operators and crawlers.</span><span class='zh'>Miasma 提供一种防御性手段，通过注入诱饵数据和路由技巧让 AI 爬虫耗费资源以达到阻止抓取的目的。它旨在抑制大规模爬虫的行为，同时暴露现有反爬策略的薄弱点，引发关于网络防御伦理与爬虫对抗的讨论。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Security' style='--c:#ef4444'>Security</span><span class='tag' data-t='Open Source' style='--c:#22c55e'>Open Source</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>1.3</span><span class='m ic-up'>302</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47561819' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>217</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-47496221" data-id="47496221" data-tags="Culture|Programming" data-score="272" data-comments="74" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47496221" data-time="1774306355">

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/antithesishq/bombadil" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bombadil: Property-based testing for web UIs</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil：Antithesis 的网页 UI 属性测试工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bombadil brings property-based testing to front-end UI testing, generating diverse interaction sequences to expose edge cases that example tests miss.</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil 为前端 UI 引入属性测试，通过自动生成各种交互序列来揭示常规测试难以覆盖的边界问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bombadil brings property-based testing to front-end UI testing, generating diverse interaction sequences to expose edge cases that example tests miss. It emphasizes automated shrinking of failing runs to minimal reproducing cases and integrates with common web stacks, helping teams catch flaky behavior before release.</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil 为前端 UI 引入属性测试，通过自动生成各种交互序列来揭示常规测试难以覆盖的边界问题。它还能对失败用例进行最小化缩减，便于定位复现，并与主流前端栈对接，帮助团队在发布前发现易出错的行为。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/chenglou/pretext" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pretext: TypeScript library for multiline text measurement and layout</span><span class='zh'>Pretext：用于多行文本测量与布局的 TypeScript 库</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pretext provides a TypeScript library for accurate multiline text measurement and layout.</span><span class='zh'>Pretext 提供一个用于多行文本测量与布局的 TypeScript 库，帮助在不同字体与字号下实现可预期的换行与高度。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pretext provides a TypeScript library for accurate multiline text measurement and layout. It helps developers render dynamic text with predictable wrapping and height, reducing layout bugs across fonts and sizes. This can be especially valuable for canvas or SVG-based UI work.</span><span class='zh'>Pretext 提供一个用于多行文本测量与布局的 TypeScript 库，帮助在不同字体与字号下实现可预期的换行与高度。对于需要精确文本渲染的场景（如画布或 SVG UI）特别有用，减少布局错误。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/facebookresearch/hyperagents" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>HyperAgents: Self-referential self-improving agents</span><span class='zh'>HyperAgents：自我指涉的自我提升代理</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>HyperAgents introduces self-referential agents that can inspect and adjust their own policies to improve performance.</span><span class='zh'>HyperAgents 介绍了能够自我审视并调整自身策略的代理，形成自我提升循环。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>HyperAgents introduces self-referential agents that can inspect and adjust their own policies to improve performance. The work explores auto-improvement loops, self-assessment, and the potential benefits and risks of self-modifying agents for complex tasks.</span><span class='zh'>HyperAgents 介绍了能够自我审视并调整自身策略的代理，形成自我提升循环。该研究探索自我评估与自我修改在解决复杂任务时的潜在收益与风险，以及对 AI 系统设计的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/t8/hypura" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hypura – A storage-tier-aware LLM inference scheduler for Apple Silicon</span><span class='zh'>Hypura — 面向 Apple Silicon 的存储层感知型 LLM 推理调度器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hypura introduces a storage-tier-aware LLM inference scheduler designed for Apple Silicon, aiming to optimize memory usage and latency by intelligently staging data between RAM and storage.</span><span class='zh'>Hypura 引入面向 Apple Silicon 的存储层感知型 LLM 推理调度器，旨在通过在 RAM 与存储之间智能分层数据来优化内存使用与延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hypura introduces a storage-tier-aware LLM inference scheduler designed for Apple Silicon, aiming to optimize memory usage and latency by intelligently staging data between RAM and storage. By accounting for the different storage tiers, it reduces memory pressure during on-device inference of large models and can boost throughput on Macs.</span><span class='zh'>Hypura 引入面向 Apple Silicon 的存储层感知型 LLM 推理调度器，旨在通过在 RAM 与存储之间智能分层数据来优化内存使用与延迟。通过考虑不同存储层级，它在本地大模型推理中降低内存压力，并有望提升 Mac 上的吞吐量。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dune3d/dune3d" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dune3d: A parametric 3D CAD application</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d：一个参数化的3D CAD应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Dune3d is an open-source parametric 3D CAD project that lets users define and modify geometry with parameters and constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d 是一个开源的参数化3D CAD项目，允许通过参数和约束来定义和修改几何形状。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dune3d is an open-source parametric 3D CAD project that lets users define and modify geometry with parameters and constraints. The project exemplifies how modular, scriptable CAD tooling could complement or challenge heavyweight CAD suites.</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d 是一个开源的参数化3D CAD项目，允许通过参数和约束来定义和修改几何形状。该项目展示了轻量、可脚本化的 CAD 工具如何挑战主流工具并实现更快速的试验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/40710" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Claude Code runs Git reset –hard origin/main against project repo every 10 mins</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 每十分钟对项目仓库执行 git reset --hard origin/main</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Claude Code appears to run git reset --hard origin/main on a project every 10 minutes, wiping local changes and history.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 每 10 分钟就对一个项目执行 git reset --hard origin/main，导致本地改动与历史被清空。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Claude Code appears to run git reset --hard origin/main on a project every 10 minutes, wiping local changes and history. This illustrates the risks of high-frequency automation in code workflows and underscores the need for safeguards, review gates, and strict version-control discipline. The episode serves as a cautionary tale for AI-assisted coding tools.</span><span class='zh'>Claude Code 每 10 分钟就对一个项目执行 git reset --hard origin/main，导致本地改动与历史被清空。这揭示了高频自动化在代码工作流中的风险，强调需要设定保护措施、审查机制和严格的版本控制纪律。此事为 AI 辅助编码工具敲响警钟。</span></p>
<div class="sfoot"><span class="tags"><span class='tag' data-t='Programming' style='--c:#3b82f6'>Programming</span><span class='tag' data-t='AI' style='--c:#8b5cf6'>AI</span></span><span class='metas'><span class='m hot ic-fire'>4.4</span><span class='m ic-up'>212</span><a class='m ic-chat' href='https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47567969' target='_blank' rel='noopener'>138</a></span></div></div></article><article class="story xcode" hidden id="story-47493657" data-id="47493657" data-tags="Open Source|Programming" data-score="204" data-comments="121" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47493657" data-time="1774292228">
<a class="thumb" href="https://github.com/localstack/localstack" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><img class='th' src='https://repository-images.githubusercontent.com/71948498/7a7abf27-e02e-4544-8d0f-0a8630e5b77b' alt='' loading='lazy' referrerpolicy='no-referrer' data-d='github.com' data-c='#22c55e' data-tag='Open Source'></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">177</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/localstack/localstack" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Local Stack Archived their GitHub repo and requires an account to run</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 将仓库归档，运行需账户</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LocalStack has archived its GitHub repository and now requires you to sign in to run the software locally.</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 已将其 GitHub 仓库归档，现在运行需要账户登录。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LocalStack has archived its GitHub repository and now requires you to sign in to run the software locally. This marks a shift from open development toward a gated model, potentially affecting developers who rely on a free, self-hosted AWS-like environment. The move invites questions about accessibility and sustainability of community-focused open-source projects.</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 已将其 GitHub 仓库归档，现在运行需要账户登录。这标志着从开放开发向受限访问的转变，可能影响依赖免费本地 AWS 模拟环境的开发者。此举也引发关于社区开源项目可访问性与可持续性的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Velxio 2.0 is a browser-based emulation tool that lets you run Arduino, ESP32, and Raspberry Pi 3 environments directly in the browser.</span><span class='zh'>Velxio 2.0 是一个基于浏览器的仿真工具，允许在浏览器中直接运行 Arduino、ESP32 和 Raspberry Pi 3 的环境。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Velxio 2.0 is a browser-based emulation tool that lets you run Arduino, ESP32, and Raspberry Pi 3 environments directly in the browser. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/russellromney/turbolite" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Turbolite – a SQLite VFS serving sub-250ms cold JOIN queries from S3</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Turbolite——将 SQLite VFS 与 S3 集成，实现冷数据下子 250ms 的 JOIN 查询</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Turbolite demonstrates a SQLite VFS that serves JOIN queries directly from S3 with sub-250ms cold-start latency.</span><span class='zh'>Turbolite 展示了一个将 SQLite VFS 与 S3 直接结合的方案，在冷启动下实现子 250ms 的 JOIN 查询。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Turbolite demonstrates a SQLite VFS that serves JOIN queries directly from S3 with sub-250ms cold-start latency. This approach enables fast analytical queries on object storage without a traditional DBMS, hinting at cost-efficient serverless data workflows and new caching strategies.</span><span class='zh'>Turbolite 展示了一个将 SQLite VFS 与 S3 直接结合的方案，在冷启动下实现子 250ms 的 JOIN 查询。该思路为对象存储上的快速分析查询提供了新路径，可能推动无服务器数据工作流和缓存策略的发展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dbrll/ATTN-11" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Paper Tape Is All You Need – Training a Transformer on a 1976 Minicomputer</span><span class='zh'>纸带就够用——在1976年迷你计算机上训练 Transformer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
