<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A demonstration of training a Transformer on a 1976 minicomputer using paper tape and extremely limited memory. The project highlights how far AI tooling has evolved and what it takes to run modern models on vintage hardware, challenging assumptions about sets of resources required.</span><span class='zh'>在1976年的迷你计算机上，靠纸带等极端受限的资源训练 Transformer。这个演示凸显了 AI 工具与基础设施的飞跃，以及在复古硬件上运行现代模型所需的极端资源挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ben-j-c/verilog2factorio" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A Verilog to Factorio Compiler and Simulator (Working RISC-V CPU)</span><span class='zh'>Verilog 转 Factorio 的编译器与模拟器（可工作的 RISC-V CPU）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Verilog to Factorio compiler translates digital hardware descriptions into Factorio logic, enabling a working RISC-V CPU to be simulated inside the game.</span><span class='zh'>Verilog 转 Factorio 的编译器把数字硬件描述转为 Factorio 逻辑，使游戏中的 RISC-V CPU 可被仿真。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Verilog to Factorio compiler translates digital hardware descriptions into Factorio logic, enabling a working RISC-V CPU to be simulated inside the game. This opens up a playful yet educational avenue for hardware prototyping, reveals performance constraints of software simulators, and demonstrates creative cross-domain tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Verilog 转 Factorio 的编译器把数字硬件描述转为 Factorio 逻辑，使游戏中的 RISC-V CPU 可被仿真。这为硬件原型设计提供了一种有趣且具教育意义的方式，也揭示了软件仿真在性能上的限制，同时展示跨领域工具的创造力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AmElmo/proofshot" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: ProofShot – Give AI coding agents eyes to verify the UI they build</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: ProofShot – 让 AI 编码代理具备验证 UI 的能力</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ProofShot enables AI coding agents to inspect and verify the UI they generate, acting as a visual verifier for automated UI construction.</span><span class='zh'>ProofShot 让 AI 编码代理能够检查并验证它们生成的 UI，作为对自动化 UI 构建的可视化校验工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ProofShot enables AI coding agents to inspect and verify the UI they generate, acting as a visual verifier for automated UI construction. This reduces misalignment between intended design and rendered output, accelerating trusted AI-driven UI workflows. It showcases a practical approach to injecting human-in-the-loop feedback into automated UI pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>ProofShot 让 AI 编码代理能够检查并验证它们生成的 UI，作为对自动化 UI 构建的可视化校验工具。它减少了设计意图与最终呈现之间的错配，加速了 AI 驱动的 UI 工作流程中的信任建立。展示了将人类在环反馈引入自动化 UI 流程的实际方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/DavidHavoc/ayaFlow" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AyaFlow: A high-performance, eBPF-based network traffic analyzer written in Rust</span><span class='zh'>AyaFlow：用 Rust 编写的高性能基于 eBPF 的网络流量分析器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AyaFlow is a high-performance, Rust-based network traffic analyzer built on eBPF, enabling fast, safe, kernel-level visibility.</span><span class='zh'>AyaFlow 是一个用 Rust 编写、基于 eBPF 的高性能网络流量分析器，提供快速且安全的内核级可观测性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AyaFlow is a high-performance, Rust-based network traffic analyzer built on eBPF, enabling fast, safe, kernel-level visibility. It emphasizes memory safety and throughput for large-scale observability tasks. The project showcases Rust’s role in safe, efficient infrastructure tooling.</span><span class='zh'>AyaFlow 是一个用 Rust 编写、基于 eBPF 的高性能网络流量分析器，提供快速且安全的内核级可观测性。该工具强调内存安全与高吞吐，适合大规模观测任务，体现了 Rust 在基础设施工具中的应用潜力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>DuckDB gains a community extension that prefilters HNSW with ACORN-1 to speed up vector search inside analytics workloads.</span><span class='zh'>DuckDB 获得一个社区扩展，将 ACORN-1 的预筛选 HNSW 引入以加速分析工作负载中的向量检索。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>DuckDB gains a community extension that prefilters HNSW with ACORN-1 to speed up vector search inside analytics workloads. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/wolfssl/wolfguard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WolfGuard: WireGuard with FIPS 140-3 cryptography</span><span class='zh'>WolfGuard：带 FIPS 140‑3 加密的 WireGuard</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WolfGuard is a WireGuard-focused project that implements FIPS 140-3 cryptography, aligning VPN tech with government-grade security standards.</span><span class='zh'>WolfGuard 是一个聚焦于 WireGuard 的项目，实现了 FIPS 140-3 加密，确保 VPN 技术符合政府等级的安全标准。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WolfGuard is a WireGuard-focused project that implements FIPS 140-3 cryptography, aligning VPN tech with government-grade security standards. This makes it appealing for regulated industries or sensitive deployments, though it may come with certification overhead and potential compatibility considerations with standard WireGuard deployments.</span><span class='zh'>WolfGuard 是一个聚焦于 WireGuard 的项目，实现了 FIPS 140-3 加密，确保 VPN 技术符合政府等级的安全标准。对合规行业或敏感部署具有吸引力，但可能伴随认证成本和与常规 WireGuard 部署的兼容性挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/EverMind-AI/MSA" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MSA: Memory Sparse Attention</span><span class='zh'>MSA：记忆稀疏注意力</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MSA introduces memory-sparse attention to reduce memory and compute for long-context models, enabling scalable attention with less quadratic cost.</span><span class='zh'>MSA 提出记忆稀疏注意力，降低长上下文模型的内存与计算需求，使注意力计算更具可扩展性，或促使更大上下文的训练与推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MSA introduces memory-sparse attention to reduce memory and compute for long-context models, enabling scalable attention with less quadratic cost. This can impact how we design and train larger sequences.</span><span class='zh'>MSA 提出记忆稀疏注意力，降低长上下文模型的内存与计算需求，使注意力计算更具可扩展性，或促使更大上下文的训练与推理。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A primer aimed at software engineers to demystify machine learning, clarifying capabilities, limits, and practical integration patterns.</span><span class='zh'>面向软件工程师的 ML 入门，去除神秘感，澄清能力、边界与實践落地的整合模式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A primer aimed at software engineers to demystify machine learning, clarifying capabilities, limits, and practical integration patterns. It pushes back on hype and offers actionable guidance for engineering teams adopting ML in production. The piece emphasizes pragmatic, real-world ML applications over magic.</span><span class='zh'>面向软件工程师的 ML 入门，去除神秘感，澄清能力、边界与實践落地的整合模式。文章抵制噱头，给出在生产环境中采用 ML 的可执行建议，强调务实的、真实世界的应用而非幻想。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ricardoquesada/regenerator2000" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A 6502 disassembler with a TUI: A modern take on Regenerator</span><span class='zh'>带 TUI 的 6502 反汇编器：Regenerator 的现代版本</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A modern Regenerator-inspired 6502 disassembler with a terminal UI, making retro reverse engineering approachable for hobbyists and researchers.</span><span class='zh'>受 Regenerator 启发的带有终端 UI 的 6502 反汇编器，为爱好者与研究者提供更易上手的复古逆向工程工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A modern Regenerator-inspired 6502 disassembler with a terminal UI, making retro reverse engineering approachable for hobbyists and researchers.</span><span class='zh'>受 Regenerator 启发的带有终端 UI 的 6502 反汇编器，为爱好者与研究者提供更易上手的复古逆向工程工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/TechEmpower/FrameworkBenchmarks/issues/10932" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sunsetting the Techempower Framework Benchmarks</span><span class='zh'>停用 Techempower 框架基准</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Techempower Framework Benchmarks project is being sunset, ending active maintenance and updates.</span><span class='zh'>Techempower 框架基准项目将进入停止维护阶段，正式结束主动更新。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Techempower Framework Benchmarks project is being sunset, ending active maintenance and updates. The move reflects the ongoing maintenance burden and evolving ecosystems, nudging developers to rely on maintained benchmarks or run their own tests while retaining historical performance insights.</span><span class='zh'>Techempower 框架基准项目将进入停止维护阶段，正式结束主动更新。此举反映出维护负担与生态变化的压力，促使开发者改为使用受维护的基准集或自行执行性能测试，同时保留历史性能快照作为参考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/jer-irl/threadprocs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Threadprocs – executables sharing one address space (0-copy pointers)</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Threadprocs — 在同一地址空间共享可执行文件（零拷贝指针）</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Threadprocs enables multiple executables to share a single address space, enabling zero-copy data sharing and fast IPC.</span><span class='zh'>Threadprocs 允许多个可执行文件共用一个地址空间，实现零拷贝的数据共享与快速进程间通信。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Threadprocs enables multiple executables to share a single address space, enabling zero-copy data sharing and fast IPC. The approach challenges traditional process isolation and could influence future runtime and OS design.</span><span class='zh'>Threadprocs 允许多个可执行文件共用一个地址空间，实现零拷贝的数据共享与快速进程间通信。这一思路挑战传统进程隔离，可能影响未来的运行时与操作系统设计。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/openyak/desktop" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenYak – An open-source Cowork that runs any model and owns your filesystem</span><span class='zh'>OpenYak：一个开源的协作工具，能运行任意模型并掌控你的文件系统</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenYak is an open-source coworking platform that can run any machine learning model and store data on the user&#x27;s own filesystem.</span><span class='zh'>OpenYak 是一个开源的协作平台，能够在本地运行任意模型并将数据存放在用户自有的文件系统中。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenYak is an open-source coworking platform that can run any machine learning model and store data on the user&#x27;s own filesystem. Its architecture highlights data ownership and local experimentation, avoiding vendor lock-in.</span><span class='zh'>OpenYak 是一个开源的协作平台，能够在本地运行任意模型并将数据存放在用户自有的文件系统中。此设计强调数据所有权和本地试验，避免对单一厂商的依赖。</span></p>
